190:   <!-- Scripts -->

194: 
195:   <script>
196:     function renderWishlist() {
197:       if (typeof STRIDE_DATA === 'undefined') return;
198: 
199:       const grid = document.getElementById('wishlist-grid');
200:       const emptyView = document.getElementById('empty-wishlist-view');
201:       const actionBtns = document.getElementById('wishlist-action-btns');
202: 
203:       const ids = STRIDE_APP.getWishlist();
204:       const products = STRIDE_DATA.products.filter(p => ids.includes(p.id));
205: 
206:       if (products.length === 0) {
207:         grid.style.display = 'none';
208:         emptyView.style.display = 'block';
209:         actionBtns.style.display = 'none';
210:         return;
211:       }
212: 
213:       grid.style.display = 'grid';
214:       emptyView.style.display = 'none';
215:       actionBtns.style.display = 'flex';
216: 
217:       grid.innerHTML = products.map(p => `
218:         <div class="product-card">
219:           <div class="product-card-thumb">
220:             <span class="badge badge-grail" style="position: absolute; top: 1rem; left: 1rem;">${p.condition}</span>
221:             <button class="product-card-fav active" onclick="removeFromWishlist('${p.id}')" title="Remove">
222:               ✕
223:             </button>
224:             <img src="${p.image}" alt="${p.name}" class="product-card-img">
225:           </div>
226:           <div class="product-card-body">
227:             <div class="product-card-brand">${p.brand} • ${p.releaseYear}</div>
228:             <h3 class="product-card-title">${p.name}</h3>
229:             <div class="product-card-meta">
230:               <div class="product-card-price">
231:                 <span class="price-val" data-price-usd="${p.price}">${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(p.price)}</span>
232:               </div>
233:               <button class="btn btn-primary product-card-cta" onclick="moveToBag('${p.id}')">
234:                 MOVE TO BAG
235:               </button>
236:             </div>
237:           </div>
238:         </div>
239:       `).join('');
240: 
241:       STRIDE_INTERACTIONS.initCardTilt();
242:     }
243: 
244:     window.removeFromWishlist = function(id) {
245:       STRIDE_APP.toggleWishlist(id);
246:       renderWishlist();
247:     };
248: 
249:     window.moveToBag = function(id) {
250:       const p = STRIDE_DATA.products.find(item => item.id === id);
251:       if (p) {
252:         STRIDE_APP.addToCart(p, "US 10", p.condition);
253:         STRIDE_APP.toggleWishlist(id);
254:         renderWishlist();
255:         // Open drawer
256:         document.querySelector('.cart-drawer').classList.add('open');
257:         document.querySelector('.cart-drawer-backdrop').classList.add('open');
258:       }
259:     };
260: 
261:     document.getElementById('move-all-cart-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
262:       const ids = STRIDE_APP.getWishlist();
263:       ids.forEach(id => {
264:         const p = STRIDE_DATA.products.find(item => item.id === id);
265:         if (p) STRIDE_APP.addToCart(p, "US 10", p.condition);
266:       });
267:       localStorage.setItem('stride_wishlist', JSON.stringify([]));
268:       STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();
269:       renderWishlist();
270:       STRIDE_APP.showToast('All grails moved to shopping bag!', 'success');
271:       document.querySelector('.cart-drawer').classList.add('open');
272:       document.querySelector('.cart-drawer-backdrop').classList.add('open');
273:     });
274: 
275:     document.getElementById('clear-wishlist-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
276:       localStorage.setItem('stride_wishlist', JSON.stringify([]));
277:       STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();
278:       renderWishlist();
279:       STRIDE_APP.showToast('Wishlist cleared', 'info');
280:     });
281: 
282:     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
283:       renderWishlist();
284:       window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderWishlist);
285:     });
286:   </script>
287: </body>
288: </html>

234:     function renderCartPage() {
235:       const cart = STRIDE_APP.getCart();
236:       const container = document.getElementById('cart-items-container');
237:       const emptyView = document.getElementById('cart-empty-view');
238:       const checkoutBtn = document.getElementById('proceed-checkout-btn');
239: 
240:       if (cart.length === 0) {
241:         container.style.display = 'none';
242:         emptyView.style.display = 'block';
243:         checkoutBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
244:         checkoutBtn.style.opacity = '0.5';
245:         updateSummary(0);
246:         return;
247:       }
248: 
249:       container.style.display = 'block';
250:       emptyView.style.display = 'none';
251:       checkoutBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
252:       checkoutBtn.style.opacity = '1';
253: 
254:       container.innerHTML = cart.map(item => `
255:         <div class="cart-item-row">
256:           <img src="${item.image}" alt="${item.name}" class="cart-item-img">
257:           <div>
258:             <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #FF5A1F; text-transform: uppercase;">${item.brand} • ${item.condition}</div>
259:             <h4 style="font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 800; color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.3;">${item.name}</h4>
260:             <div style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: #A0A4AE; margin-top: 0.25rem;">Size: <strong style="color: #FFFFFF;">${item.size}</strong></div>
261:           </div>
262:           <div class="quantity-control">
263:             <button class="quantity-btn" onclick="updateQty('${item.id}', '${item.size}', -1)">-</button>
264:             <span class="quantity-display">${item.qty}</span>
265:             <button class="quantity-btn" onclick="updateQty('${item.id}', '${item.size}', 1)">+</button>
266:           </div>
267:           <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 800; text-align: right;">
268:             ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * item.qty)}
269:           </div>
270:           <button onclick="removeItem('${item.id}', '${item.size}')" style="color: #626775; font-size: 1.1rem; cursor: pointer;" title="Remove Item">✕</button>
271:         </div>
272:       `).join('');
273: 
274:       const subtotal = STRIDE_APP.getCartSubtotal();
275:       updateSummary(subtotal);
276: 
277:       // Shipping Progress
278:       const shippingMsg = document.getElementById('cart-page-shipping-msg');
279:       const shippingFill = document.getElementById('cart-page-shipping-fill');
280:       if (subtotal >= 200) {
281:         shippingFill.style.width = '100%';
282:         shippingMsg.innerHTML = `⚡ You qualify for <strong>FREE Insured Express Shipping!</strong>`;
283:       } else {
284:         const remaining = 200 - subtotal;
285:         shippingFill.style.width = `${Math.min(100, (subtotal / 200) * 100)}%`;
286:         shippingMsg.innerHTML = `Add <strong>${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(remaining)}</strong> more to qualify for Free Express Insured Shipping.`;
287:       }
288:     }
289: 
290:     function updateSummary(subtotal) {
291:       const subtotalEl = document.getElementById('summary-subtotal');
292:       const shippingEl = document.getElementById('summary-shipping');
293:       const totalEl = document.getElementById('summary-total');
294:       const discountRow = document.getElementById('summary-discount-row');
295:       const discountLabel = document.getElementById('discount-label');
296:       const discountEl = document.getElementById('summary-discount');
297: 
298:       subtotalEl.textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(subtotal);
299: 
300:       const shipping = subtotal >= 200 || subtotal === 0 ? 0 : 25;
301:       shippingEl.textContent = shipping === 0 ? 'FREE' : STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(shipping);
302: 
303:       const promo = STRIDE_APP.getActiveDiscount();
304:       let discountAmount = 0;
305: 
306:       if (promo.code && subtotal > 0) {
307:         discountAmount = promo.amount || 0;
308:         discountRow.style.display = 'flex';
309:         discountLabel.textContent = promo.code;
310:         discountEl.textContent = `-${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(discountAmount)}`;
311:       } else {
312:         discountRow.style.display = 'none';
313:       }
314: 
315:       const total = Math.max(0, subtotal + shipping - discountAmount);
316:       totalEl.textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(total);
317:     }
318: 
319:     window.updateQty = function(id, size, change) {
320:       STRIDE_APP.updateQuantity(id, size, change);
321:       renderCartPage();
322:     };
323: 
324:     window.removeItem = function(id, size) {
325:       STRIDE_APP.removeFromCart(id, size);
326:       renderCartPage();
327:     };
328: 
329:     document.getElementById('apply-promo-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
330:       const input = document.getElementById('promo-code-input');
331:       if (STRIDE_APP.applyPromoCode(input.value)) {
332:         renderCartPage();
333:       }
334:     });
335: 
336:     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
337:       renderCartPage();
338:       window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderCartPage);
339:     });
340:   </script>
341: </body>
342: </html>

249:       itemsList.innerHTML = cart.map(item => `

254:             <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #FF5A1F;">${item.size} • Qty: ${item.qty}</div>
255:           </div>
256:           <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 800; font-size: 0.95rem; color: #FFFFFF;">
257:             ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * item.qty)}
258:           </div>
259:         </div>
260:       `).join('');
261: 
262:       const subtotal = STRIDE_APP.getCartSubtotal();
263:       const promo = STRIDE_APP.getActiveDiscount();
264:       let discountAmount = promo.amount || 0;
265: 
266:       document.getElementById('checkout-subtotal').textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(subtotal);
267: 
268:       if (discountAmount > 0) {
269:         document.getElementById('checkout-discount-row').style.display = 'flex';
270:         document.getElementById('checkout-discount').textContent = `-${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(discountAmount)}`;
271:       }
272: 
273:       const total = Math.max(0, subtotal - discountAmount);
274:       document.getElementById('checkout-total').textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(total);
275:     }

278:       renderCheckoutSummary();
279:       window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderCheckoutSummary);
280:     });
281: 
282:     // Form Validation & Place Order
283:     document.getElementById('checkout-form').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
284:       e.preventDefault();
285:       let valid = true;
286: 
287:       const email = document.getElementById('checkout-email');
288:       const phone = document.getElementById('checkout-phone');
289:       const firstName = document.getElementById('first-name');
290:       const lastName = document.getElementById('last-name');
291:       const address = document.getElementById('address');
292:       const city = document.getElementById('city');
293:       const state = document.getElementById('state');
294:       const zip = document.getElementById('zip');
295: 
296:       const inputs = [email, phone, firstName, lastName, address, city, state, zip];
297:       inputs.forEach(input => {
298:         if (!input.value.trim()) {
299:           input.parentElement.classList.add('error');
300:           valid = false;
301:         } else {
302:           input.parentElement.classList.remove('error');
303:         }
304:       });
305: 
306:       if (valid) {
307:         const orderId = `STR-${Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)}`;
308:         const cart = STRIDE_APP.getCart();
309:         const subtotal = STRIDE_APP.getCartSubtotal();
310: 
311:         const newOrder = {
312:           orderId: orderId,
313:           date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }),
314:           status: 'Authenticated by Stride',
315:           total: subtotal,
316:           carrier: 'FedEx Express Priority',
317:           trackingNumber: `FX-${Math.floor(100000000 + Math.random() * 900000000)}-US`,
318:           estimatedDelivery: 'In 2 Business Days',
319:           currentStep: 1,
320:           items: cart,
321:           shippingAddress: {
322:             name: `${firstName.value} ${lastName.value}`,
323:             address: address.value,
324:             city: city.value,
325:             state: state.value,
326:             zip: zip.value,
327:             country: 'United States'
328:           }
329:         };
330: 
331:         // Save order to localStorage
332:         let orders = [];
333:         try {
334:           const stored = localStorage.getItem('stride_orders');
335:           orders = stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : [];
336:         } catch(e) {}
337:         orders.unshift(newOrder);
338:         localStorage.setItem('stride_orders', JSON.stringify(orders));
339: 
340:         // Clear cart
341:         localStorage.setItem('stride_cart', JSON.stringify([]));
342:         localStorage.removeItem('stride_promo_code');
343:         STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();
344: 
345:         // Redirect to Order Confirmation
346:         window.location.href = `order-confirmation.html?orderId=${orderId}`;
347:       }
348:     });

17: // Parse URL params (e.g. ?brand=Jordan or ?filter=grail)
18: function parseUrlParams() {
19:   const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
20:   const brandParam = params.get('brand');
21:   const filterParam = params.get('filter');
22: 
23:   if (brandParam) {
24:     selectedBrands.push(brandParam);
25:     const checkbox = document.querySelector(`input[name="brand"][value="${brandParam}"]`);
26:     if (checkbox) checkbox.checked = true;
27:   }
28: 
29:   if (filterParam === 'grail') {
30:     selectedConditions.push('Deadstock');
31:   }
32: }
33: 
34: function renderCatalogue() {
35:   if (typeof STRIDE_DATA === 'undefined') return;
36: 
37:   let list = [...STRIDE_DATA.products];
38: 
39:   // 1. Search Query
40:   if (searchQuery) {
41:     const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
42:     list = list.filter(p => 
43:       p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
44:       p.brand.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
45:       p.sku.toLowerCase().includes(q)
46:     );
47:   }
48: 
49:   // 2. Brand Filter
50:   if (selectedBrands.length > 0) {
51:     list = list.filter(p => selectedBrands.includes(p.brand));
52:   }
53: 
54:   // 3. Condition Filter
55:   if (selectedConditions.length > 0) {
56:     list = list.filter(p => selectedConditions.includes(p.condition));
57:   }
58: 
59:   // 4. Max Price Slider
60:   list = list.filter(p => p.price <= maxPrice);
61: 
62:   // 5. Sorting
63:   if (sortMode === 'price-low') {
64:     list.sort((a, b) => a.price - b.price);
65:   } else if (sortMode === 'price-high') {
66:     list.sort((a, b) => b.price - a.price);
67:   } else if (sortMode === 'newest') {
68:     list.sort((a, b) => b.releaseYear - a.releaseYear);
69:   } else if (sortMode === 'rating') {
70:     list.sort((a, b) => b.rating - a.rating);
71:   }
72: 
73:   countEl.textContent = list.length;
74: 
75:   if (list.length === 0) {
76:     grid.style.display = 'none';
77:     noResults.style.display = 'block';
78:     return;
79:   }
80: 
81:   grid.style.display = 'grid';
82:   noResults.style.display = 'none';
83: 
84:   grid.innerHTML = list.map(p => {
85:     const isFav = STRIDE_APP.isWishlisted(p.id);
86:     const badgeClass = p.badge === 'GRAIL' ? 'badge-grail' : (p.condition === 'Deadstock' ? 'badge-deadstock' : 'badge-vnds');
87: 
88:     return `
89:       <div class="product-card">
90:         <div class="product-card-thumb">
91:           <div class="product-card-badges">
92:             <span class="badge ${badgeClass}">${p.badge || p.condition}</span>
93:             <span class="badge badge-verified">🛡️ VERIFIED</span>
94:           </div>
95:           <button 
96:             class="product-card-fav ${isFav ? 'active' : ''}" 
97:             data-wishlist-id="${p.id}" 
98:             onclick="STRIDE_APP.toggleWishlist('${p.id}')"
99:             aria-label="Save to Wishlist"

/**
 * STRIDE — CORE APPLICATION CONTROLLER
 * Reactive Cart, Wishlist, Search Engine, Drawer, Toasts, Navigation

const STRIDE_APP = {
  // Determine if running from /pages/ or root
  isPagesDir: window.location.pathname.includes('/pages/'),

  getRootPath(path) {
    if (this.isPagesDir) {
      return path.startsWith('/') ? '..' + path : '../' + path;
    }
    return path;
  },

  getPagePath(page) {
    if (this.isPagesDir) {
      return page;
    }
    return 'pages/' + page;
  },

  // --- REGIONAL CURRENCY & PRICE CONVERTER ENGINE ---
  getCurrency() {

    return (typeof STRIDE_DATA !== 'undefined' && STRIDE_DATA.currencies) ? STRIDE_DATA.currencies : {

      GBP: { code: "GBP", name: "GBP (£)", symbol: "£", rate: 0.79, flag: "🇬🇧", region: "United Kingdom" },
      CAD: { code: "CAD", name: "CAD (CA$)", symbol: "CA$", rate: 1.36, flag: "🇨🇦", region: "Canada" },
      KWD: { code: "KWD", name: "KWD (KD)", symbol: "KD ", rate: 0.31, flag: "🇰🇼", region: "Kuwait / Middle East" }
    };
  },

  getCurrencyData() {
    const currencies = this.getCurrencies();
    const code = this.getCurrency();
    return currencies[code] || currencies['USD'] || { code: "USD", name: "USD ($)", symbol: "$", rate: 1.0, flag: "🇺🇸", region: "Global" };
  },

  formatPrice(usdAmount) {
    if (usdAmount === null || usdAmount === undefined || isNaN(usdAmount)) return '$0';
    const num = Number(usdAmount);
    const curr = this.getCurrencyData();
    const converted = Math.round(num * curr.rate);
    const formatted = converted.toLocaleString();
    return `${curr.symbol}${formatted}`;
  },

  setCurrency(code) {
    const currencies = this.getCurrencies();
    if (!currencies[code]) return;
    localStorage.setItem('stride_currency', code);
    const curr = currencies[code];

    // Synchronize all currency selectors / badges on page
    document.querySelectorAll('.currency-trigger-label').forEach(el => {
      el.innerHTML = `${curr.flag} <span>${curr.code} (${curr.symbol.trim()})</span>`;
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.currency-select-input').forEach(sel => {
      sel.value = code;
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.currency-dropdown-option').forEach(opt => {
      if (opt.getAttribute('data-code') === code) {
        opt.classList.add('active');
      } else {
        opt.classList.remove('active');
      }
    });

    // Update all elements with data-price-usd
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-price-usd]').forEach(el => {
      const usd = parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-price-usd'));
      if (!isNaN(usd)) {
        el.textContent = this.formatPrice(usd);
      }
    });

    // Update cart drawer
    this.renderCartDrawer();

    // Broadcast custom event for pages with specialized catalogs (e.g. shop.html, cart.html)
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('stride:currencychange', {

    let cart = this.getCart();
    const existingIndex = cart.findIndex(item => item.id === product.id && item.size === size);

        size: size,
        qty: qty
      });
    }

    this.saveCart(cart);
    this.showToast(`Added ${product.name} (${size}) to bag!`, 'success');
  },

  // --- HEADER SCROLL DETECTOR ---
  initHeaderScroll() {
    const header = document.querySelector('.stride-header');
    if (!header) return;

    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
      if (window.scrollY > 30) {
        header.classList.add('scrolled');
      } else {
        header.classList.remove('scrolled');
      }
    });
  },

  // --- MOBILE HAMBURGER MENU ---
  initMobileMenu() {
    const toggle = document.querySelector('.mobile-menu-toggle');
    const drawer = document.querySelector('.mobile-nav');
    const overlay = document.querySelector('.mobile-nav-overlay');
    const closeBtn = document.querySelector('.mobile-nav-close');

    if (!toggle || !drawer) return;

    const openMenu = () => {
      drawer.classList.add('open');
      if (overlay) overlay.classList.add('open');
    };

    const closeMenu = () => {
      drawer.classList.remove('open');
      if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('open');
    };

    toggle.addEventListener('click', openMenu);
    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);
    if (overlay) overlay.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);
  },

  // --- BOOTSTRAP MASTER INIT ---
  init() {
    this.initCurrencySelector();
    this.updateBadges();
    this.initHeaderScroll();
    this.initMobileMenu();
    this.initCartDrawer();
    this.initSearchModal();
  }

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  STRIDE_APP.init();

/**
 * STRIDE — 3D VISUALS & INTERACTIVE EFFECTS
 * 3D Card Tilt, Hero Sneaker Parallax, Ambient Particle Canvas, Quick View Modal
 */

const STRIDE_INTERACTIONS = {
  // --- 1. 3D PRODUCT CARD TILT ---
  initCardTilt() {
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.product-card');

    cards.forEach(card => {
      card.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
        const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
        const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
        const y = e.clientY - rect.top;

        const centerX = rect.width / 2;
        const centerY = rect.height / 2;

        const rotateX = ((y - centerY) / centerY) * -8; // max 8 deg
        const rotateY = ((x - centerX) / centerX) * 8;

        card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg) translateY(-8px) scale(1.02)`;
      });

      card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
        card.style.transform = 'perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg) translateY(0px) scale(1)';
      });
    });
  },

  // --- 2. HERO SNEAKER 3D PARALLAX & LIGHTING ---
  initHeroParallax() {
    const hero = document.querySelector('.hero-section');
    const sneakerWrap = document.querySelector('.hero-sneaker-wrapper');
    const glowOrb = document.querySelector('.sneaker-glow-orb');

    if (!hero || !sneakerWrap) return;

    hero.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
      const rect = hero.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width - 0.5;
      const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height - 0.5;

      const tiltX = -y * 22; // 22 deg tilt
      const tiltY = x * 28;

      sneakerWrap.style.transform = `rotateX(${tiltX}deg) rotateY(${tiltY}deg) translateZ(30px)`;

      if (glowOrb) {
        glowOrb.style.transform = `translate(${x * 60}px, ${y * 60}px) scale(1.08)`;
      }
    });

    hero.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
      sneakerWrap.style.transform = 'rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg) translateZ(0px)';
      if (glowOrb) glowOrb.style.transform = 'translate(0, 0) scale(1)';
    });
  },

  // --- 3. AMBIENT PARTICLES CANVAS ---
  initParticles() {
    const canvas = document.querySelector('.hero-particles-canvas');
    if (!canvas) return;

    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    let width = (canvas.width = canvas.parentElement.offsetWidth);
    let height = (canvas.height = canvas.parentElement.offsetHeight);

    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
      if (!canvas.parentElement) return;
      width = canvas.width = canvas.parentElement.offsetWidth;
      height = canvas.height = canvas.parentElement.offsetHeight;
    });

    const particles = [];
    const particleCount = Math.min(45, Math.floor(width / 30));

    for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
      particles.push({
        x: Math.random() * width,
        y: Math.random() * height,
        radius: Math.random() * 2 + 0.8,
        vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4,
        vy: -Math.random() * 0.7 - 0.2, // Drift upward
        alpha: Math.random() * 0.6 + 0.2,
        color: Math.random() > 0.4 ? 'rgba(255, 90, 31,' : 'rgba(255, 140, 66,'
      });
    }

    function render() {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

      particles.forEach(p => {
        p.x += p.vx;
        p.y += p.vy;

        if (p.y < -10) {
          p.y = height + 10;
          p.x = Math.random() * width;
        }
        if (p.x < -10) p.x = width + 10;
        if (p.x > width + 10) p.x = -10;

        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = `${p.color} ${p.alpha})`;
        ctx.shadowBlur = 8;
        ctx.shadowColor = '#FF5A1F';
        ctx.fill();
      });

      requestAnimationFrame(render);
    }

    render();
  },

  // --- 4. QUICK VIEW MODAL ---
  openQuickView(productId) {
    if (typeof STRIDE_DATA === 'undefined') return;
    const product = STRIDE_DATA.products.find(p => p.id === productId);
    if (!product) return;

    let modal = document.querySelector('.quickview-modal-backdrop');
    if (!modal) {
      modal = document.createElement('div');
      modal.className = 'quickview-modal-backdrop search-modal-backdrop';
      document.body.appendChild(modal);
    }

    const isPages = window.location.pathname.includes('/pages/');
    const pdpUrl = isPages ? `product-detail.html?id=${product.id}` : `pages/product-detail.html?id=${product.id}`;

    modal.innerHTML = `
      <div class="search-modal-box" style="max-width: 780px; padding: 2rem; position: relative;">
        <button class="close-quickview" style="position: absolute; top: 1.25rem; right: 1.5rem; color: #A0A4AE; font-size: 1.2rem; cursor: pointer;">✕</button>
        <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1.2fr; gap: 2rem; align-items: center;">
          <div style="background: radial-gradient(circle, #1F2430 0%, #151821 100%); border-radius: 14px; padding: 2rem; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;">
            <img src="${product.image}" alt="${product.name}" style="max-height: 220px; object-fit: contain; filter: drop-shadow(0 15px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.8));">
          </div>
          <div>
            <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #FF5A1F; text-transform: uppercase; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 0.35rem;">${product.brand} • ${product.condition}</div>
            <h3 style="font-size: 1.4rem; font-weight: 800; margin-bottom: 0.75rem; line-height: 1.2;">${product.name}</h3>
            <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1.6rem; font-weight: 900; color: #FFFFFF; margin-bottom: 1rem;">$${product.price.toLocaleString()}</div>
            <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: #A0A4AE; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; line-height: 1.5;">${product.description.slice(0, 140)}...</p>
            
            <div style="margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">
              <label style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; text-transform: uppercase; color: #A0A4AE; display: block; margin-bottom: 0.4rem;">Select Size</label>
              <select id="quickview-size-select" class="custom-select" style="width: 100%;">
                <option value="US 8">US 8 • $${product.price.toLocaleString()}</option>
                <option value="US 8.5">US 8.5 • $${product.price.toLocaleString()}</option>
                <option value="US 9">US 9 • $${product.price.toLocaleString()}</option>
                <option value="US 9.5">US 9.5 • $${product.price.toLocaleString()}</option>
                <option value="US 10" selected>US 10 • $${product.price.toLocaleString()}</option>
                <option value="US 10.5">US 10.5 • $${product.price.toLocaleString()}</option>
                <option value="US 11">US 11 • $${product.price.toLocaleString()}</option>
                <option value="US 12">US 12 • $${product.price.toLocaleString()}</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.75rem;">
              <button id="quickview-add-cart-btn" class="btn btn-primary" style="flex-grow: 1;">Add to Bag</button>
              <a href="${pdpUrl}" class="btn btn-secondary">Full Details</a>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    modal.classList.add('open');

    modal.querySelector('.close-quickview').addEventListener('click', () => modal.classList.remove('open'));
    modal.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === modal) modal.classList.remove('open');
    });

    document.getElementById('quickview-add-cart-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const selectedSize = document.getElementById('quickview-size-select').value;
      STRIDE_APP.addToCart(product, selectedSize, product.condition);
      modal.classList.remove('open');
    });
  },

  init() {
    this.initCardTilt();
    this.initHeroParallax();
    this.initParticles();
  }
};

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  STRIDE_INTERACTIONS.init();
});

121:   addToCart(product, size = "US 10", condition = "Deadstock", qty = 1) {

I inspected your three assigned pages in [SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers) along with the shared stylesheets and scripts on the `main` branch (`css/stride.css`, `css/stride-pages.css`, `js/stride-data.js`, `js/stride-core.js`, `js/stride-interactions.js`). Nothing has been modified.

---

### What Is Already Working (Designed & Wired)

1. **Shared Engine (`main` branch `js/stride-core.js` & `js/stride-data.js`)**
   - `STRIDE_APP` manages `localStorage` persistence for `stride_cart`, `stride_wishlist`, `stride_promo_code`, and `stride_currency`.
   - Built-in promo engine supports `STRIDE10` (10% off) and `FIRSTDROP` (\$50 off).
   - Regional currency selector auto-injects into `.nav-actions` and `.mobile-nav-links`, updates `[data-price-usd]` elements, and dispatches `stride:currencychange`.
   - Shared header scroll state, mobile drawer, search modal, cart drawer, badge counters, and toast notifications are all wired.

2. **[wishlist.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/wishlist.html) (Page 10)**
   - Renders saved items from `STRIDE_APP.getWishlist()` + `STRIDE_DATA.products` with condition badge, `✕` remove button, price, and `MOVE TO BAG` button.
   - Includes **Move All to Bag**, **Clear Wishlist**, `#empty-wishlist-view` empty state, 3D card tilt (`STRIDE_INTERACTIONS.initCardTilt()`), and currency change re-rendering.

3. **[cart.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/cart.html) (Page 11)**
   - Renders cart rows with thumbnail, brand/condition, size, `-`/`+` quantity steppers, line total, and `✕` remove button.
   - Includes the **\$200 Free Express Shipping progress bar**, empty-cart state (disabling checkout), **Order Summary** card, and promo code input.

4. **[checkout.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/checkout.html) (Page 12)**
   - Redirects to `cart.html` if the bag is empty, renders the sticky order breakdown, validates required contact/address fields on submit, saves the new order to `localStorage` (`stride_orders`), clears `stride_cart` and `stride_promo_code`, and redirects to `order-confirmation.html?orderId=...`.

---

### What Needs Attention

1. **Missing Shared Files on Current Branch (`i263197`)**
   - Your working branch `i263197` does not currently have `css/`, `js/`, or `assets/logo.svg` / `assets/logo-mark.svg` (those exist on `main`, while `i263197` has `Aazar*.css`/`Aazar*.js` at the root). Until `main` is merged into your branch, all CSS, JS, and logo references on your 3 pages will fail to load.

2. **[checkout.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/checkout.html) Bugs & Missing Features**
   - **Payment tabs are static:** Clicking **Apple Pay / Google Pay** or **STRIDE Vault Balance (Crypto / USDC)** does not switch `.active`, check the radio input, or hide/toggle `#card-fields`.
   - **Shipping & Total math:** Selecting **STRIDE Armored Courier (\$45.00)** does not update `#checkout-shipping` or `#checkout-total`, and `renderCheckoutSummary()` ignores shipping fees completely (including standard \$25 shipping when subtotal < \$200). On submit, `newOrder.total` saves `subtotal` instead of the final discounted/shipped total, and `carrier` is hardcoded to FedEx.
   - **Form validation & fields:** Validation only checks for non-empty strings (no email format, phone format, or card field validation when Card is selected), and the **Country** field shown in the blueprint wireframe is missing.
   - **Mobile layout:** The main 2-column grid (`grid-template-columns: 1.4fr 1fr`) and 3-column city/state/zip row use inline styles with no mobile media query, so they will overflow on narrow screens.

3. **[cart.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/cart.html) Bugs & Responsiveness**
   - **Progress bar on empty cart:** When the last item is removed, `renderCartPage()` returns early before resetting `#cart-page-shipping-fill` (`0%`) and `#cart-page-shipping-msg`.
   - **Mobile row layout:** `.cart-item-row` (`grid-template-columns: 100px 1.8fr 120px 100px 40px` in `stride-pages.css`) has no mobile breakpoint, causing horizontal overflow on phones.
   - **Promo UX:** Pressing `Enter` in `#promo-code-input` does not trigger Apply.

4. **[wishlist.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/wishlist.html) Polish & Alignment**
   - **Saved count subtitle:** Both your paper prototype and the blueprint show a dynamic count (e.g., `(3 Saved Pairs)`), which is currently missing from the header subtitle.
   - **Move to Bag behavior:** Hardcodes `"US 10"` and fires two overlapping toasts (`Added to bag` + `Removed from Wishlist`) on individual moves.

5. **Semantic HTML5 & Accessibility (All 3 Pages)**
   - Primary page content should be wrapped in `<main>` (currently `<div>` / `<section>`), icon-only buttons (`✕`, `-`, `+`) need `aria-label` attributes, and `checkout.html` is missing the shared footer/announcement bar if required by your group's shared UI kit.

Let me know when you're ready for me to start fixing these (and whether you'd like to merge/sync `main`'s `css/`, `js/`, and `assets/` first).

## MODEL (Step 40 — `2026-11-27T18:10:27Z`)

I inspected and fixed the payment tabs, shipping options, and total calculation in [checkout.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/checkout.html) without altering the page layout.

### What Was Broken
1. **Payment Tabs (`#tab-card`, `#tab-apple`, `#tab-crypto`)**: There were no click/change handlers attached, so clicking Apple Pay or Crypto didn't check the radio button, move the `.active` state, or hide `#card-fields`.
2. **Shipping Options**: Selecting **STRIDE Armored Courier (\$45.00)** checked the radio input but didn't update `.active` on the shipping cards, `#checkout-shipping`, or `#checkout-total`.
3. **Total Calculation & Saved Order**: `renderCheckoutSummary()` ignored shipping fees (both the \$25 standard fee under \$200 and the \$45 overnight fee), didn't hide `#checkout-discount-row` when discount was `0`, and form submission saved `subtotal` as `newOrder.total` with a hardcoded FedEx carrier.

### What Was Fixed in [checkout.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/checkout.html)
- **Payment Tab Switching (`initPaymentTabs`)**: Clicking any payment tab updates the radio selection, toggles `.active` across the payment tabs, shows/hides `#card-fields` when `card` is selected, and toggles `required` + validates card inputs (`#card-num`, `#card-exp`, `#card-cvc`) only when `card` is active.
- **Shipping Toggle & Live Totals (`initShippingOptions`, `getShippingCost`, `renderCheckoutSummary`)**:
  - Switching between `standard` (\$0 if subtotal ≥ \$200, otherwise \$25) and `overnight` (\$45) updates `.active` on `[data-shipping-tab]`, updates the option price labels and `#checkout-shipping`, hides/shows `#checkout-discount-row`, and calculates `total = Math.max(0, subtotal + shippingCost - discountAmount)`.
  - On submit, `newOrder` now stores the calculated `total`, `paymentMethod`, selected `carrier`, and `estimatedDelivery`.
- Verified the inline `<script>` parses cleanly via `node` (`JS syntax OK`).

*(Note: Live browser rendering still depends on merging `css/`, `js/`, and `assets/` from `main` into your `i263197` branch.)*

Would you like to check **cart.html** or **wishlist.html** next?

discountAmount);\n\n        const newOrder = {\n          orderId: orderId,\n          date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }),\n          status: 'Authenticated by Stride',\n          total: total,\n          paymentMethod: getSelectedPaymentMethod(),\n          carrier: shippingMethod === 'overnight' ? ## MODEL (Step 95 — `2026-11-27T19:24:27Z`)

All three of your assigned pages are now updated, verified, and wired to the shared `css/`, `js/`, and `assets/` files on branch `i263197`:

### 1. [wishlist.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/wishlist.html) (Page 10 — Saved Grails)
- Wrapped main content in semantic `<main>`.
- Added a live saved-pairs counter subtitle (`#wishlist-count-subtitle`, e.g., `3 Saved Pairs • Synchronized across all browser sessions.`).
- Fixed `moveToBag(id)` so moving an item to the bag removes it from `stride_wishlist` and updates badges without firing a duplicate "Removed from Wishlist" toast.
- Added `aria-label` to the remove button on each saved card.

### 2. [cart.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/cart.html) (Page 11 — Shopping Bag)
- Wrapped main content in semantic `<main>`.
- Added responsive mobile CSS (`@media (max-width: 768px)`) so `.cart-item-row` reflows cleanly without horizontal overflow on phones.
- Fixed the empty-cart edge case so `#cart-page-shipping-fill` resets to `0%` and `#cart-page-shipping-msg` resets to the `$200` threshold message when the last item is removed.
- Added `Enter`-key support on `#promo-code-input` and `aria-label` attributes on `-`, `+`, and `✕` buttons.

### 3. [checkout.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/checkout.html) (Page 12 — Checkout & Payment)
- Wrapped main content in semantic `<main>` and added a compact footer.
- Added responsive grid rules (`.checkout-grid`, `.checkout-name-row`, `.checkout-city-row`, `.checkout-summary-card`) for tablet and mobile screens.
- Fixed payment-tab switching (`#tab-card`, `#tab-apple`, `#tab-crypto`) with dedicated `#apple-fields` and `#crypto-fields` panels, active state sync, and conditional `required` card validation.
- Fixed shipping option toggling (`standard` vs `overnight`) and `renderCheckoutSummary()` so switching back and forth recalculates `total = Math.max(0, subtotal + shippingCost - discountAmount)` cleanly without stacking `$45`.
- Added the **Country** field (`#country`) and stronger validation (email format, 7+ digit phone, card number/expiry/CVC when `card` is selected, focus on first invalid field + error toast), and saved the accurate `total`, `paymentMethod`, `carrier`, `estimatedDelivery`, and `country` to `stride_orders`.

### Verification
- Confirmed via `node` that all inline `<script>` blocks in `pages/wishlist.html`, `pages/cart.html`, and `pages/checkout.html` parse without syntax errors, all three include `<main>`, and all shared `css/`, `js/`, and `assets/` files exist in the repository.

196: 
197:   <script>
198:     function renderWishlist() {
199:       if (typeof STRIDE_DATA === 'undefined') return;
200: 
201:       const grid = document.getElementById('wishlist-grid');
202:       const emptyView = document.getElementById('empty-wishlist-view');
203:       const actionBtns = document.getElementById('wishlist-action-btns');
204:       const countSubtitle = document.getElementById('wishlist-count-subtitle');
205: 
206:       const ids = STRIDE_APP.getWishlist();
207:       const products = STRIDE_DATA.products.filter(p => ids.includes(p.id));
208: 
209:       if (countSubtitle) {
210:         const label = products.length === 1 ? '1 Saved Pair' : `${products.length} Saved Pairs`;
211:         countSubtitle.textContent = `${label} • Synchronized across all browser sessions.`;
212:       }
213: 
214:       if (products.length === 0) {
215:         grid.style.display = 'none';
216:         emptyView.style.display = 'block';
217:         actionBtns.style.display = 'none';
218:         return;
219:       }
220: 
221:       grid.style.display = 'grid';
222:       emptyView.style.display = 'none';
223:       actionBtns.style.display = 'flex';
224: 
225:       grid.innerHTML = products.map(p => `
226:         <div class="product-card">
227:           <div class="product-card-thumb">
228:             <span class="badge badge-grail" style="position: absolute; top: 1rem; left: 1rem;">${p.condition}</span>
229:             <button class="product-card-fav active" onclick="removeFromWishlist('${p.id}')" title="Remove" aria-label="Remove ${p.name} from wishlist">
230:               ✕
231:             </button>
232:             <img src="${p.image}" alt="${p.name}" class="product-card-img">
233:           </div>
234:           <div class="product-card-body">
235:             <div class="product-card-brand">${p.brand} • ${p.releaseYear}</div>
236:             <h3 class="product-card-title">${p.name}</h3>
237:             <div class="product-card-meta">
238:               <div class="product-card-price">
239:                 <span class="price-val" data-price-usd="${p.price}">${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(p.price)}</span>
240:               </div>
241:               <button class="btn btn-primary product-card-cta" onclick="moveToBag('${p.id}')">
242:                 MOVE TO BAG
243:               </button>
244:             </div>
245:           </div>
246:         </div>
247:       `).join('');
248: 
249:       STRIDE_INTERACTIONS.initCardTilt();
250:     }
251: 
252:     window.removeFromWishlist = function(id) {
253:       STRIDE_APP.toggleWishlist(id);
254:       renderWishlist();
255:     };
256: 
257:     window.moveToBag = function(id) {
258:       const p = STRIDE_DATA.products.find(item => item.id === id);
259:       if (p) {
260:         STRIDE_APP.addToCart(p, "US 10", p.condition);
261:         const updatedWishlist = STRIDE_APP.getWishlist().filter(itemId => itemId !== id);
262:         localStorage.setItem('stride_wishlist', JSON.stringify(updatedWishlist));
263:         STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();
264:         renderWishlist();
265:         // Open drawer
266:         document.querySelector('.cart-drawer').classList.add('open');
267:         document.querySelector('.cart-drawer-backdrop').classList.add('open');
268:       }
269:     };
270: 
271:     document.getElementById('move-all-cart-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
272:       const ids = STRIDE_APP.getWishlist();
273:       ids.forEach(id => {
274:         const p = STRIDE_DATA.products.find(item => item.id === id);
275:         if (p) STRIDE_APP.addToCart(p, "US 10", p.condition);
276:       });
277:       localStorage.setItem('stride_wishlist', JSON.stringify([]));
278:       STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();
279:       renderWishlist();
280:       STRIDE_APP.showToast('All grails moved to shopping bag!', 'success');
281:       document.querySelector('.cart-drawer').classList.add('open');
282:       document.querySelector('.cart-drawer-backdrop').classList.add('open');
283:     });
284: 
285:     document.getElementById('clear-wishlist-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
286:       localStorage.setItem('stride_wishlist', JSON.stringify([]));
287:       STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();
288:       renderWishlist();
289:       STRIDE_APP.showToast('Wishlist cleared', 'info');
290:     });
291: 
292:     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
293:       renderWishlist();
294:       window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderWishlist);
295:     });
296:   </script>
297: </body>
298: </html>

241:       </div>
242:     </div>
243:   </footer>
244: 
245:   <!-- Search Modal -->
246:   <div class="search-modal-backdrop">
247:     <div class="search-modal-box">
248:       <div class="search-input-wrap">
249:         <input type="text" class="search-input" placeholder="Search by model, brand, or SKU...">
250:         <button class="close-search">✕</button>
251:       </div>
252:       <div class="search-results-list"></div>
253:     </div>
254:   </div>
255: 
256:   <!-- Scripts -->
257:   <script src="../js/stride-data.js"></script>
258:   <script src="../js/stride-core.js"></script>
259: 
260:   <script>
261:     function renderCartPage() {
262:       const cart = STRIDE_APP.getCart();
263:       const container = document.getElementById('cart-items-container');
264:       const emptyView = document.getElementById('cart-empty-view');
265:       const checkoutBtn = document.getElementById('proceed-checkout-btn');
266: 
267:       const shippingMsg = document.getElementById('cart-page-shipping-msg');
268:       const shippingFill = document.getElementById('cart-page-shipping-fill');
269: 
270:       if (cart.length === 0) {
271:         container.style.display = 'none';
272:         emptyView.style.display = 'block';
273:         checkoutBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
274:         checkoutBtn.style.opacity = '0.5';
275:         checkoutBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
276:         shippingFill.style.width = '0%';
277:         shippingMsg.innerHTML = `Add <strong>${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(200)}</strong> more to qualify for Free Express Insured Shipping.`;
278:         updateSummary(0);
279:         return;
280:       }
281: 
282:       container.style.display = 'block';
283:       emptyView.style.display = 'none';
284:       checkoutBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
285:       checkoutBtn.style.opacity = '1';
286:       checkoutBtn.removeAttribute('aria-disabled');
287: 
288:       container.innerHTML = cart.map(item => `
289:         <div class="cart-item-row">
290:           <img src="${item.image}" alt="${item.name}" class="cart-item-img">
291:           <div>
292:             <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #FF5A1F; text-transform: uppercase;">${item.brand} • ${item.condition}</div>
293:             <h4 style="font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 800; color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.3;">${item.name}</h4>
294:             <div style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: #A0A4AE; margin-top: 0.25rem;">Size: <strong style="color: #FFFFFF;">${item.size}</strong></div>
295:           </div>
296:           <div class="quantity-control">
297:             <button class="quantity-btn" onclick="updateQty('${item.id}', '${item.size}', -1)" aria-label="Decrease quantity of ${item.name}">-</button>
298:             <span class="quantity-display">${item.qty}</span>
299:             <button class="quantity-btn" onclick="updateQty('${item.id}', '${item.size}', 1)" aria-label="Increase quantity of ${item.name}">+</button>
300:           </div>
301:           <div class="cart-item-price" style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 800; text-align: right;">
302:             ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * item.qty)}
303:           </div>
304:           <button class="cart-item-remove" onclick="removeItem('${item.id}', '${item.size}')" style="color: #626775; font-size: 1.1rem; cursor: pointer;" title="Remove Item" aria-label="Remove ${item.name} from bag">✕</button>
305:         </div>
306:       `).join('');
307: 
308:       const subtotal = STRIDE_APP.getCartSubtotal();
309:       updateSummary(subtotal);
310: 
311:       // Shipping Progress
312:       if (subtotal >= 200) {
313:         shippingFill.style.width = '100%';
314:         shippingMsg.innerHTML = `⚡ You qualify for <strong>FREE Insured Express Shipping!</strong>`;
315:       } else {
316:         const remaining = 200 - subtotal;
317:         shippingFill.style.width = `${Math.min(100, (subtotal / 200) * 100)}%`;
318:         shippingMsg.innerHTML = `Add <strong>${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(remaining)}</strong> more to qualify for Free Express Insured Shipping.`;
319:       }
320:     }
321: 
322:     function updateSummary(subtotal) {
323:       const subtotalEl = document.getElementById('summary-subtotal');
324:       const shippingEl = document.getElementById('summary-shipping');
325:       const totalEl = document.getElementById('summary-total');
326:       const discountRow = document.getElementById('summary-discount-row');
327:       const discountLabel = document.getElementById('discount-label');
328:       const discountEl = document.getElementById('summary-discount');
329: 
330:       subtotalEl.textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(subtotal);
331: 
332:       const shipping = subtotal >= 200 || subtotal === 0 ? 0 : 25;
333:       shippingEl.textContent = shipping === 0 ? 'FREE' : STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(shipping);
334: 
335:       const promo = STRIDE_APP.getActiveDiscount();
336:       let discountAmount = 0;
337: 
338:       if (promo.code && subtotal > 0) {
339:         discountAmount = promo.amount || 0;
340:         discountRow.style.display = 'flex';
341:         discountLabel.textContent = promo.code;
342:         discountEl.textContent = `-${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(discountAmount)}`;
343:       } else {
344:         discountRow.style.display = 'none';
345:       }
346: 
347:       const total = Math.max(0, subtotal + shipping - discountAmount);
348:       totalEl.textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(total);
349:     }
350: 
351:     window.updateQty = function(id, size, change) {
352:       STRIDE_APP.updateQuantity(id, size, change);
353:       renderCartPage();
354:     };
355: 
356:     window.removeItem = function(id, size) {
357:       STRIDE_APP.removeFromCart(id, size);
358:       renderCartPage();
359:     };
360: 
361:     document.getElementById('apply-promo-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
362:       const input = document.getElementById('promo-code-input');
363:       if (STRIDE_APP.applyPromoCode(input.value)) {
364:         renderCartPage();
365:       }
366:     });
367: 
368:     document.getElementById('promo-code-input').addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
369:       if (e.key === 'Enter') {
370:         e.preventDefault();
371:         document.getElementById('apply-promo-btn').click();
372:       }
373:     });
374: 
375:     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
376:       renderCartPage();
377:       window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderCartPage);
378:     });
379:   </script>
380: </body>
381: </html>

305:   <script>
306:     function getSelectedShippingMethod() {
307:       const checked = document.querySelector('input[name="shipping-method"]:checked');
308:       return checked ? checked.value : 'standard';
309:     }
310: 
311:     function getSelectedPaymentMethod() {
312:       const checked = document.querySelector('input[name="payment-method"]:checked');
313:       return checked ? checked.value : 'card';
314:     }
315: 
316:     function getShippingCost(subtotal) {
317:       if (getSelectedShippingMethod() === 'overnight') {
318:         return 45;
319:       }
320:       return subtotal >= 200 || subtotal === 0 ? 0 : 25;
321:     }
322: 
323:     function initShippingOptions() {
324:       const shippingRadios = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="shipping-method"]');
325:       shippingRadios.forEach(radio => {
326:         radio.addEventListener('change', () => {
327:           document.querySelectorAll('[data-shipping-tab]').forEach(tab => {
328:             tab.classList.toggle('active', tab.querySelector('input') === radio);
329:           });
330:           renderCheckoutSummary();
331:         });
332:       });
333:     }
334: 
335:     function initPaymentTabs() {
336:       const paymentTabs = [
337:         document.getElementById('tab-card'),
338:         document.getElementById('tab-apple'),
339:         document.getElementById('tab-crypto')
340:       ].filter(Boolean);
341:       const paymentPanels = {
342:         card: document.getElementById('card-fields'),

344:         crypto: document.getElementById('crypto-fields')
345:       };

347:         .map(id => document.getElementById(id))
348:         .filter(Boolean);
349: 
350:       function syncPaymentUI(selectedMethod) {
351:         paymentTabs.forEach(tab => {
352:           const radio = tab.querySelector('input[name="payment-method"]');
353:           const isSelected = radio && radio.value === selectedMethod;
354:           if (radio) radio.checked = isSelected;
355:           tab.classList.toggle('active', isSelected);
356:         });
357: 
358:         Object.entries(paymentPanels).forEach(([method, panel]) => {
359:           if (panel) panel.style.display = method === selectedMethod ? 'block' : 'none';
360:         });
361: 
362:         const isCard = selectedMethod === 'card';
363:         cardInputs.forEach(input => {
364:           input.required = isCard;
365:           if (!isCard) input.parentElement.classList.remove('error');
366:         });
367:       }
368: 
369:       paymentTabs.forEach(tab => {
370:         const radio = tab.querySelector('input[name="payment-method"]');
371:         tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
372:           if (radio) syncPaymentUI(radio.value);
373:         });
374:         if (radio) {
375:           radio.addEventListener('change', () => syncPaymentUI(radio.value));
376:         }
377:       });
378: 
379:       syncPaymentUI(getSelectedPaymentMethod());
380:     }
381: 
382:     function renderCheckoutSummary() {
383:       const cart = STRIDE_APP.getCart();

385:         window.location.href = 'cart.html';

397:             <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #FF5A1F;">${item.size} • Qty: ${item.qty}</div>
398:           </div>
399:           <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 800; font-size: 0.95rem; color: #FFFFFF;">
400:             ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * item.qty)}
401:           </div>
402:         </div>
403:       `).join('');
404: 
405:       const subtotal = STRIDE_APP.getCartSubtotal();
406:       const standardCost = subtotal >= 200 || subtotal === 0 ? 0 : 25;
407:       const shippingCost = getShippingCost(subtotal);
408:       const promo = STRIDE_APP.getActiveDiscount();
409:       const discountAmount = promo.amount || 0;
410: 
411:       const stdOptionPrice = document.getElementById('shipping-option-standard-price');
412:       if (stdOptionPrice) {
413:         stdOptionPrice.textContent = standardCost === 0 ? 'FREE' : STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(standardCost);
414:         stdOptionPrice.style.color = standardCost === 0 ? '#34D399' : '#FFFFFF';
415:       }

418:         overnightOptionPrice.textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(45);

422: 
423:       const shippingEl = document.getElementById('checkout-shipping');

425:       shippingEl.style.color = shippingCost === 0 ? '#34D399' : '#FFFFFF';
426:

428:       if (discountAmount > 0) {
429:         discountRow.style.display = 'flex';
430:         document.getElementById('checkout-discount').textContent = `-${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(discountAmount)}`;
431:       } else {
432:         discountRow.style.display = 'none';
433:       }
434: 
435:       const total = Math.max(0, subtotal + shippingCost - discountAmount);
436:       document.getElementById('checkout-total').textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(total);
437:     }

446:     // Form Validation & Place Order
447:     document.getElementById('checkout-form').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
448:       e.preventDefault();
449:       let valid = true;
450:       let firstInvalid = null;
451: 
452:       const email = document.getElementById('checkout-email');
453:       const phone = document.getElementById('checkout-phone');
454:       const firstName = document.getElementById('first-name');
455:       const lastName = document.getElementById('last-name');
456:       const address = document.getElementById('address');
457:       const city = document.getElementById('city');

459:       const zip = document.getElementById('zip');
460:       const country = document.getElementById('country');
461: 
462:       const emailPattern = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
463:       const phoneDigits = phone.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
464: 
465:       const checks = [
466:         { input: email, ok: emailPattern.test(email.value.trim()) },
467:         { input: phone, ok: phoneDigits.length >= 7 },
468:         { input: firstName, ok: firstName.value.trim().length > 0 },
469:         { input: lastName, ok: lastName.value.trim().length > 0 },
470:         { input: address, ok: address.value.trim().length > 0 },
471:         { input: city, ok: city.value.trim().length > 0 },
472:         { input: state, ok: state.value.trim().length > 0 },
473:         { input: zip, ok: zip.value.trim().length > 0 },
474:         { input: country, ok: country.value.trim().length > 0 }

478:         const cardNum = document.getElementById('card-num');
479:         const cardExp = document.getElementById('card-exp');
480:         const cardCvc = document.getElementById('card-cvc');
481:         checks.push(
482:           { input: cardNum, ok: cardNum.value.trim().length >= 12 },
483:           { input: cardExp, ok: /^\d{2}\/\d{2}$/.test(cardExp.value.trim()) },
484:           { input: cardCvc, ok: /^\d{3,4}$/.test(cardCvc.value.trim()) }
485:         );
486:       }
487: 
488:       checks.forEach(({ input, ok }) => {
489:         if (!ok) {
490:           input.parentElement.classList.add('error');
491:           if (!firstInvalid) firstInvalid = input;
492:           valid = false;
493:         } else {
494:           input.parentElement.classList.remove('error');
495:         }
496:       });
497: 
498:       if (!valid) {
499:         if (firstInvalid) firstInvalid.focus();
500:         STRIDE_APP.showToast('Please complete all required checkout fields.', 'error');
501:         return;
502:       }
503: 
504:       if (valid) {
505:         const orderId = `STR-${Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)}`;
506:         const cart = STRIDE_APP.getCart();
507:         const subtotal = STRIDE_APP.getCartSubtotal();
508:         const shippingMethod = getSelectedShippingMethod();
509:         const shippingCost = getShippingCost(subtotal);
510:         const promo = STRIDE_APP.getActiveDiscount();
511:         const discountAmount = promo.amount || 0;
512:         const total = Math.max(0, subtotal + shippingCost - discountAmount);
513: 
514:         const newOrder = {
515:           orderId: orderId,
516:           date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }),
517:           status: 'Authenticated by Stride',
518:           total: total,
519:           paymentMethod: getSelectedPaymentMethod(),
520:           carrier: shippingMethod === 'overnight' ? 'STRIDE Armored Courier' : 'FedEx Express Priority',
521:           trackingNumber: `FX-${Math.floor(100000000 + Math.random() * 900000000)}-US`,
522:           estimatedDelivery: shippingMethod === 'overnight' ? 'Next Morning Delivery' : 'In 2 Business Days',
523:           currentStep: 1,
524:           items: cart,
525:           shippingAddress: {

527:             address: address.value,
528:             city: city.value,
529:             state: state.value,
530:             zip: zip.value,
531:             country: country.value.trim() || 'United States'
532:           }
533:         };
534: 
535:         // Save order to localStorage
536:         let orders = [];
537:         try {

539:           orders = stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : [];
540:         } catch(e) {}

542:         localStorage.setItem('stride_orders', JSON.stringify(orders));

  <script>
    function renderWishlist() {
      if (typeof STRIDE_DATA === 'undefined') return;

      const grid = document.getElementById('wishlist-grid');
      const emptyView = document.getElementById('empty-wishlist-view');
      const actionBtns = document.getElementById('wishlist-action-btns');
      const countSubtitle = document.getElementById('wishlist-count-subtitle');

      const ids = STRIDE_APP.getWishlist();
      const products = STRIDE_DATA.products.filter(p => ids.includes(p.id));

      if (countSubtitle) {
        const label = products.length === 1 ? '1 Saved Pair' : `${products.length} Saved Pairs`;
        countSubtitle.textContent = `${label} • Synchronized across all browser sessions.`;
      }

      if (products.length === 0) {
        grid.style.display = 'none';
        emptyView.style.display = 'block';
        actionBtns.style.display = 'none';
        return;
      }

      grid.style.display = 'grid';
      emptyView.style.display = 'none';
      actionBtns.style.display = 'flex';

      grid.innerHTML = products.map(p => `
        <div class="product-card">
          <div class="product-card-thumb">
            <span class="badge badge-grail" style="position: absolute; top: 1rem; left: 1rem;">${p.condition}</span>
            <button class="product-card-fav active" onclick="removeFromWishlist('${p.id}')" title="Remove" aria-label="Remove ${p.name} from wishlist">
              ✕
            </button>
            <img src="${p.image}" alt="${p.name}" class="product-card-img">
          </div>
          <div class="product-card-body">
            <div class="product-card-brand">${p.brand} • ${p.releaseYear}</div>
            <h3 class="product-card-title">${p.name}</h3>
            <div class="product-card-meta">
              <div class="product-card-price">
                <span class="price-val" data-price-usd="${p.price}">${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(p.price)}</span>
              </div>
              <button class="btn btn-primary product-card-cta" onclick="moveToBag('${p.id}')">
                MOVE TO BAG
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');

      STRIDE_INTERACTIONS.initCardTilt();
    }

    window.removeFromWishlist = function(id) {
      STRIDE_APP.toggleWishlist(id);
      renderWishlist();
    };

    window.moveToBag = function(id) {
      const p = STRIDE_DATA.products.find(item => item.id === id);
      if (p) {
        STRIDE_APP.addToCart(p, "US 10", p.condition);
        const updatedWishlist = STRIDE_APP.getWishlist().filter(itemId => itemId !== id);
        localStorage.setItem('stride_wishlist', JSON.stringify(updatedWishlist));
        STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();
        renderWishlist();
        // Open drawer
        document.querySelector('.cart-drawer').classList.add('open');
        document.querySelector('.cart-drawer-backdrop').classList.add('open');
      }
    };

    document.getElementById('move-all-cart-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const ids = STRIDE_APP.getWishlist();
      ids.forEach(id => {
        const p = STRIDE_DATA.products.find(item => item.id === id);
        if (p) STRIDE_APP.addToCart(p, "US 10", p.condition);
      });
      localStorage.setItem('stride_wishlist', JSON.stringify([]));
      STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();
      renderWishlist();
      STRIDE_APP.showToast('All grails moved to shopping bag!', 'success');
      document.querySelector('.cart-drawer').classList.add('open');
      document.querySelector('.cart-drawer-backdrop').classList.add('open');
    });

    document.getElementById('clear-wishlist-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      localStorage.setItem('stride_wishlist', JSON.stringify([]));
      STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();
      renderWishlist();
      STRIDE_APP.showToast('Wishlist cleared', 'info');
    });

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      renderWishlist();
      window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderWishlist);
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="../js/stride-data.js"></script>
  <script src="../js/stride-core.js"></script>

  <script>
    function renderCartPage() {
      const cart = STRIDE_APP.getCart();
      const container = document.getElementById('cart-items-container');
      const emptyView = document.getElementById('cart-empty-view');
      const checkoutBtn = document.getElementById('proceed-checkout-btn');

      const shippingMsg = document.getElementById('cart-page-shipping-msg');
      const shippingFill = document.getElementById('cart-page-shipping-fill');

      if (cart.length === 0) {
        container.style.display = 'none';
        emptyView.style.display = 'block';
        checkoutBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        checkoutBtn.style.opacity = '0.5';
        checkoutBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
        shippingFill.style.width = '0%';
        shippingMsg.innerHTML = `Add <strong>${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(200)}</strong> more to qualify for Free Express Insured Shipping.`;
        updateSummary(0);
        return;
      }

      container.style.display = 'block';
      emptyView.style.display = 'none';
      checkoutBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
      checkoutBtn.style.opacity = '1';
      checkoutBtn.removeAttribute('aria-disabled');

      container.innerHTML = cart.map(item => `
        <div class="cart-item-row">
          <img src="${item.image}" alt="${item.name}" class="cart-item-img">
          <div>
            <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #FF5A1F; text-transform: uppercase;">${item.brand} • ${item.condition}</div>
            <h4 style="font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 800; color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.3;">${item.name}</h4>
            <div style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: #A0A4AE; margin-top: 0.25rem;">Size: <strong style="color: #FFFFFF;">${item.size}</strong></div>
          </div>
          <div class="quantity-control">
            <button class="quantity-btn" onclick="updateQty('${item.id}', '${item.size}', -1)" aria-label="Decrease quantity of ${item.name}">-</button>
            <span class="quantity-display">${item.qty}</span>
            <button class="quantity-btn" onclick="updateQty('${item.id}', '${item.size}', 1)" aria-label="Increase quantity of ${item.name}">+</button>
          </div>
          <div class="cart-item-price" style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 800; text-align: right;">
            ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * item.qty)}
          </div>
          <button class="cart-item-remove" onclick="removeItem('${item.id}', '${item.size}')" style="color: #626775; font-size: 1.1rem; cursor: pointer;" title="Remove Item" aria-label="Remove ${item.name} from bag">✕</button>
        </div>
      `).join('');

      const subtotal = STRIDE_APP.getCartSubtotal();
      updateSummary(subtotal);

      // Shipping Progress
      if (subtotal >= 200) {
        shippingFill.style.width = '100%';
        shippingMsg.innerHTML = `⚡ You qualify for <strong>FREE Insured Express Shipping!</strong>`;
      } else {
        const remaining = 200 - subtotal;
        shippingFill.style.width = `${Math.min(100, (subtotal / 200) * 100)}%`;
        shippingMsg.innerHTML = `Add <strong>${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(remaining)}</strong> more to qualify for Free Express Insured Shipping.`;
      }
    }

    function updateSummary(subtotal) {
      const subtotalEl = document.getElementById('summary-subtotal');
      const shippingEl = document.getElementById('summary-shipping');
      const totalEl = document.getElementById('summary-total');
      const discountRow = document.getElementById('summary-discount-row');
      const discountLabel = document.getElementById('discount-label');
      const discountEl = document.getElementById('summary-discount');

      subtotalEl.textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(subtotal);

      const shipping = subtotal >= 200 || subtotal === 0 ? 0 : 25;
      shippingEl.textContent = shipping === 0 ? 'FREE' : STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(shipping);

      const promo = STRIDE_APP.getActiveDiscount();
      let discountAmount = 0;

      if (promo.code && subtotal > 0) {
        discountAmount = promo.amount || 0;
        discountRow.style.display = 'flex';
        discountLabel.textContent = promo.code;
        discountEl.textContent = `-${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(discountAmount)}`;
      } else {
        discountRow.style.display = 'none';
      }

      const total = Math.max(0, subtotal + shipping - discountAmount);
      totalEl.textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(total);
    }

    window.updateQty = function(id, size, change) {
      STRIDE_APP.updateQuantity(id, size, change);
      renderCartPage();
    };

    window.removeItem = function(id, size) {
      STRIDE_APP.removeFromCart(id, size);
      renderCartPage();
    };

    document.getElementById('apply-promo-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const input = document.getElementById('promo-code-input');
      if (STRIDE_APP.applyPromoCode(input.value)) {
        renderCartPage();
      }
    });

    document.getElementById('promo-code-input').addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        document.getElementById('apply-promo-btn').click();
      }
    });

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      renderCartPage();
      window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderCartPage);
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```
