### 3. [pages/checkout.html](file:///C:/Users/LATITUDE/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/checkout.html) (Page 12 — Checkout & Payment)

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>Checkout — STRIDE Secure Order Placement</title>
  <meta name="description" content="Complete your purchase with encrypted checkout, insured priority shipping, and 100% authenticity guarantee on STRIDE.">
  <link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="../assets/logo-mark.svg">
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="../css/stride.css">
  <style>
    .payment-tab {
      background: #11131A;
      border: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);
      border-radius: var(--radius-md);
      padding: 1rem 1.25rem;
      cursor: pointer;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.75rem;
      transition: all var(--transition-fast);
      margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
    }
    .payment-tab.active {
      border-color: var(--neon-orange);
      background: rgba(255, 90, 31, 0.08);
    }
    .payment-tab input[type="radio"] {
      accent-color: var(--neon-orange);
    }
    .checkout-grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1.4fr 1fr;
      gap: 3.5rem;
      align-items: flex-start;
    }
    .checkout-summary-card {
      position: sticky;
      top: 100px;
    }
    .checkout-name-row {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
      gap: 1rem;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }
    .checkout-city-row {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1.2fr 1fr 1fr;
      gap: 1rem;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }
    @media (max-width: 960px) {
      .checkout-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 2rem;
      }
      .checkout-summary-card {
        position: static;
      }
    }
    @media (max-width: 600px) {
      .checkout-name-row,
      .checkout-city-row {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
      }
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>

  <!-- Top Simple Brand Header -->
  <header style="background: #11131A; border-bottom: 1px solid #292D38; padding: 1.25rem 0;">
    <div class="container" style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;">
      <a href="../index.html" class="brand-logo">
        <img src="../assets/logo.svg" alt="STRIDE" width="150">
      </a>
      <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.65rem; font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.8rem; color: #34D399;">
        <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><rect x="3" y="11" width="18" height="11" rx="2" ry="2"/><path d="M7 11V7a5 5 0 0 1 10 0v4"/></svg>
        256-BIT ENCRYPTED CHECKOUT
      </div>
    </div>
  </header>

  <main class="container" style="padding-top: 3rem; padding-bottom: 6rem;">
    <div class="checkout-grid">
      <!-- Left: Checkout Forms -->
      <div>
        <form id="checkout-form" novalidate>
          <!-- 1. Customer Information -->
          <div class="glass-card" style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; border-bottom: 1px solid #292D38; padding-bottom: 0.75rem;">
              <h3 style="font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase;">1. Contact Information</h3>
              <a href="login.html" style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: #FF5A1F; font-family: var(--font-mono);">Already a Member? Log In</a>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group" style="margin-bottom: 1.25rem;">
              <label class="form-label" for="checkout-email">Email for Tracking Updates *</label>
              <input type="email" id="checkout-email" class="form-input" placeholder="collector@stride.com" required>
              <span class="form-error-msg">Please enter a valid email address.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label" for="checkout-phone">Mobile Phone (Delivery SMS notifications) *</label>
              <input type="tel" id="checkout-phone" class="form-input" placeholder="+1 (555) 019-2834" required>
              <span class="form-error-msg">Please provide a valid phone number.</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- 2. Delivery Address -->
          <div class="glass-card" style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">
            <h3 style="font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; border-bottom: 1px solid #292D38; padding-bottom: 0.75rem;">
              2. Delivery Address
            </h3>

            <div class="checkout-name-row">
              <div class="form-group">
                <label class="form-label" for="first-name">First Name *</label>
                <input type="text" id="first-name" class="form-input" placeholder="Alex" required>
                <span class="form-error-msg">Required.</span>
              </div>
              <div class="form-group">
                <label class="form-label" for="last-name">Last Name *</label>
                <input type="text" id="last-name" class="form-input" placeholder="Vance" required>
                <span class="form-error-msg">Required.</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group" style="margin-bottom: 1rem;">
              <label class="form-label" for="address">Street Address *</label>
              <input type="text" id="address" class="form-input" placeholder="742 Evergreen Terrace" required>
              <span class="form-error-msg">Street address is required.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group" style="margin-bottom: 1rem;">
              <label class="form-label" for="apartment">Apartment, Suite, Unit (Optional)</label>
              <input type="text" id="apartment" class="form-input" placeholder="Suite 4B">
            </div>

            <div class="checkout-city-row">
              <div class="form-group">
                <label class="form-label" for="city">City *</label>
                <input type="text" id="city" class="form-input" placeholder="San Francisco" required>
                <span class="form-error-msg">City required.</span>
              </div>
              <div class="form-group">
                <label class="form-label" for="state">State / Prov *</label>
                <input type="text" id="state" class="form-input" placeholder="CA" required>
                <span class="form-error-msg">State required.</span>
              </div>
              <div class="form-group">
                <label class="form-label" for="zip">Postal Code *</label>
                <input type="text" id="zip" class="form-input" placeholder="94107" required>
                <span class="form-error-msg">Postal code required.</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label" for="country">Country *</label>
              <input type="text" id="country" class="form-input" placeholder="United States" value="United States" required>
              <span class="form-error-msg">Country required.</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- 3. Shipping Speed -->
          <div class="glass-card" style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">
            <h3 style="font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 1.25rem;">
              3. Courier Dispatch Method
            </h3>
            <label class="payment-tab active" data-shipping-tab="standard">
              <input type="radio" name="shipping-method" value="standard" checked>
              <div style="flex-grow: 1;">
                <div style="font-weight: 700; color: #FFFFFF; font-size: 0.95rem;">FedEx Express Insured Priority (2-3 Business Days)</div>
                <div style="font-size: 0.78rem; color: #A0A4AE;">Double-boxed with tamper-evident security holographic tape</div>
              </div>
              <span id="shipping-option-standard-price" style="color: #34D399; font-weight: 800; font-family: var(--font-mono);">FREE</span>
            </label>

            <label class="payment-tab" data-shipping-tab="overnight">
              <input type="radio" name="shipping-method" value="overnight">
              <div style="flex-grow: 1;">
                <div style="font-weight: 700; color: #FFFFFF; font-size: 0.95rem;">STRIDE Armored Courier (Next Morning Delivery)</div>
                <div style="font-size: 0.78rem; color: #A0A4AE;">Dedicated courier van direct to your doorstep</div>
              </div>
              <span id="shipping-option-overnight-price" style="color: #FFFFFF; font-weight: 800; font-family: var(--font-mono);">$45.00</span>
            </label>
          </div>

          <!-- 4. Payment Simulation -->
          <div class="glass-card" style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">
            <h3 style="font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 1.25rem;">
              4. Payment Method (Demo Simulator)
            </h3>

            <div class="payment-tab active" id="tab-card">
              <input type="radio" name="payment-method" value="card" checked>
              <span style="font-weight: 700; color: #FFFFFF;">Credit / Debit Card</span>
              <span style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #A0A4AE; margin-left: auto;">Visa, MC, Amex</span>
            </div>

            <!-- Card Inputs -->
            <div id="card-fields" style="background: #11131A; border: 1px solid #292D38; border-radius: 12px; padding: 1.5rem; margin-bottom: 1rem;">
              <div class="form-group" style="margin-bottom: 1rem;">
                <label class="form-label" for="card-num">Card Number</label>
                <input type="text" id="card-num" class="form-input" placeholder="4242 •••• •••• 4242" value="4242 •••• •••• 4242" required>
              </div>
              <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 1rem;">
                <div class="form-group">
                  <label class="form-label" for="card-exp">Expiration</label>
                  <input type="text" id="card-exp" class="form-input" placeholder="12/28" value="12/28" required>
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                  <label class="form-label" for="card-cvc">Security Code</label>
                  <input type="text" id="card-cvc" class="form-input" placeholder="882" value="882" required>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="payment-tab" id="tab-apple">
              <input type="radio" name="payment-method" value="apple">
              <span style="font-weight: 700; color: #FFFFFF;">Apple Pay / Google Pay</span>
              <span style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #A0A4AE; margin-left: auto;">Biometric Touch</span>
            </div>

            <!-- Apple Pay / Google Pay Content -->
            <div id="apple-fields" style="display: none; background: #11131A; border: 1px solid #292D38; border-radius: 12px; padding: 1.5rem; margin-bottom: 1rem;">
              <div style="font-weight: 700; color: #FFFFFF; font-size: 0.92rem; margin-bottom: 0.35rem;">Biometric Express Checkout Ready</div>
              <p style="font-size: 0.82rem; color: #A0A4AE; margin: 0;">You will be prompted to confirm payment via Face ID, Touch ID, or Google Pay passkey when you click <strong style="color: #FFFFFF;">Place Order &amp; Secure Grail</strong>.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="payment-tab" id="tab-crypto">
              <input type="radio" name="payment-method" value="crypto">
              <span style="font-weight: 700; color: #FFFFFF;">STRIDE Vault Balance (Crypto / USDC)</span>
              <span style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #FF5A1F; margin-left: auto;">Instant Settlement</span>
            </div>

            <!-- Crypto / USDC Content -->
            <div id="crypto-fields" style="display: none; background: #11131A; border: 1px solid #292D38; border-radius: 12px; padding: 1.5rem; margin-bottom: 0.25rem;">
              <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 0.35rem;">
                <span style="font-weight: 700; color: #FFFFFF; font-size: 0.92rem;">USDC Instant Vault Settlement</span>
                <span style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #34D399;">0% NETWORK FEE</span>
              </div>
              <p style="font-size: 0.82rem; color: #A0A4AE; margin: 0;">Settle instantly using your STRIDE Vault USDC balance with zero gas fees and immediate 8-Point Lab queue priority.</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-lg btn-block" style="padding: 1.25rem;">
            PLACE ORDER & SECURE GRAIL
            <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><polyline points="9 18 15 12 9 6"/></svg>
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <!-- Right: Live Order Breakdown -->
      <aside class="glass-card checkout-summary-card">
        <h3 style="font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; border-bottom: 1px solid #292D38; padding-bottom: 0.75rem;">
          In Your Bag (<span id="checkout-item-count">0</span>)
        </h3>

        <div id="checkout-items-list" style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 1rem; max-height: 280px; overflow-y: auto; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; border-bottom: 1px solid #292D38; padding-bottom: 1.25rem;">
          <!-- Dynamically populated items -->
        </div>

        <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.8rem; font-size: 0.9rem; color: #A0A4AE;">
          <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
            <span>Subtotal:</span>
            <span id="checkout-subtotal" style="color: #FFFFFF; font-weight: 700;">$0</span>
          </div>
          <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
            <span>Shipping:</span>
            <span id="checkout-shipping" style="color: #34D399; font-weight: 700;">FREE</span>
          </div>
          <div id="checkout-discount-row" style="display: none; justify-content: space-between; color: #FF5A1F; font-weight: 700;">
            <span>Discount:</span>
            <span id="checkout-discount">-$0</span>
          </div>
          <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; border-top: 1px solid #292D38; padding-top: 1rem; font-size: 1.3rem; font-weight: 900; color: #FFFFFF;">
            <span>Total:</span>
            <span id="checkout-total" style="color: var(--neon-orange);">$0</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style="margin-top: 1.5rem; padding: 1rem; background: #11131A; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid #292D38; font-size: 0.8rem; color: #A0A4AE; display: flex; gap: 0.75rem; align-items: center;">
          <svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#FF5A1F" stroke-width="2"><path d="M12 22s8-4 8-10V5l-8-3-8 3v7c0 6 8 10 8 10z"/></svg>
          <div>
            <strong style="color: #FFFFFF;">100% Buyer Shield Guarantee:</strong> Full refund if inspection fails.
          </div>
        </div>
      </aside>
    </div>
  </main>

  <footer class="stride-footer" style="padding: 2rem 0;">
    <div class="container">
      <div class="footer-bottom" style="border-top: none; padding-top: 0;">
        <div>&copy; 2026 STRIDE Marketplace Inc. All rights reserved.</div>
        <div>Tokyo • New York • London • Paris</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </footer>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="../js/stride-data.js"></script>
  <script src="../js/stride-core.js"></script>

  <script>
    function getSelectedShippingMethod() {
      const checked = document.querySelector('input[name="shipping-method"]:checked');
      return checked ? checked.value : 'standard';
    }

    function getSelectedPaymentMethod() {
      const checked = document.querySelector('input[name="payment-method"]:checked');
      return checked ? checked.value : 'card';
    }

    function getShippingCost(subtotal) {
      if (getSelectedShippingMethod() === 'overnight') {
        return 45;
      }
      return subtotal >= 200 || subtotal === 0 ? 0 : 25;
    }

    function initShippingOptions() {
      const shippingRadios = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="shipping-method"]');
      shippingRadios.forEach(radio => {
        radio.addEventListener('change', () => {
          document.querySelectorAll('[data-shipping-tab]').forEach(tab => {
            tab.classList.toggle('active', tab.querySelector('input') === radio);
          });
          renderCheckoutSummary();
        });
      });
    }

    function initPaymentTabs() {
      const paymentTabs = [
        document.getElementById('tab-card'),
        document.getElementById('tab-apple'),
        document.getElementById('tab-crypto')
      ].filter(Boolean);
      const paymentPanels = {
        card: document.getElementById('card-fields'),
        apple: document.getElementById('apple-fields'),
        crypto: document.getElementById('crypto-fields')
      };
      const cardInputs = ['card-num', 'card-exp', 'card-cvc']
        .map(id => document.getElementById(id))
        .filter(Boolean);

      function syncPaymentUI(selectedMethod) {
        paymentTabs.forEach(tab => {
          const radio = tab.querySelector('input[name="payment-method"]');
          const isSelected = radio && radio.value === selectedMethod;
          if (radio) radio.checked = isSelected;
          tab.classList.toggle('active', isSelected);
        });

        Object.entries(paymentPanels).forEach(([method, panel]) => {
          if (panel) panel.style.display = method === selectedMethod ? 'block' : 'none';
        });

        const isCard = selectedMethod === 'card';
        cardInputs.forEach(input => {
          input.required = isCard;
          if (!isCard) input.parentElement.classList.remove('error');
        });
      }

      paymentTabs.forEach(tab => {
        const radio = tab.querySelector('input[name="payment-method"]');
        tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (radio) syncPaymentUI(radio.value);
        });
        if (radio) {
          radio.addEventListener('change', () => syncPaymentUI(radio.value));
        }
      });

      syncPaymentUI(getSelectedPaymentMethod());
    }

    function renderCheckoutSummary() {
      const cart = STRIDE_APP.getCart();
      if (cart.length === 0) {
        window.location.href = 'cart.html';
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('checkout-item-count').textContent = cart.reduce((s, i) => s + i.qty, 0);

      const itemsList = document.getElementById('checkout-items-list');
      itemsList.innerHTML = cart.map(item => `
        <div style="display: flex; gap: 1rem; align-items: center;">
          <img src="${item.image}" alt="${item.name}" style="width: 58px; height: 50px; object-fit: contain; background: #11131A; border-radius: 8px; padding: 4px;">
          <div style="flex-grow: 1;">
            <div style="font-weight: 700; color: #FFFFFF; font-size: 0.85rem; line-height: 1.2;">${item.name}</div>
            <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #FF5A1F;">${item.size} • Qty: ${item.qty}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 800; font-size: 0.95rem; color: #FFFFFF;">
            ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * item.qty)}
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');

      const subtotal = STRIDE_APP.getCartSubtotal();
      const standardCost = subtotal >= 200 || subtotal === 0 ? 0 : 25;
      const shippingCost = getShippingCost(subtotal);
      const promo = STRIDE_APP.getActiveDiscount();
      const discountAmount = promo.amount || 0;

      const stdOptionPrice = document.getElementById('shipping-option-standard-price');
      if (stdOptionPrice) {
        stdOptionPrice.textContent = standardCost === 0 ? 'FREE' : STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(standardCost);
        stdOptionPrice.style.color = standardCost === 0 ? '#34D399' : '#FFFFFF';
      }
      const overnightOptionPrice = document.getElementById('shipping-option-overnight-price');
      if (overnightOptionPrice) {
        overnightOptionPrice.textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(45);
      }

      document.getElementById('checkout-subtotal').textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(subtotal);

      const shippingEl = document.getElementById('checkout-shipping');
      shippingEl.textContent = shippingCost === 0 ? 'FREE' : STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(shippingCost);
      shippingEl.style.color = shippingCost === 0 ? '#34D399' : '#FFFFFF';

      const discountRow = document.getElementById('checkout-discount-row');
      if (discountAmount > 0) {
        discountRow.style.display = 'flex';
        document.getElementById('checkout-discount').textContent = `-${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(discountAmount)}`;
      } else {
        discountRow.style.display = 'none';
      }

      const total = Math.max(0, subtotal + shippingCost - discountAmount);
      document.getElementById('checkout-total').textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(total);
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      initShippingOptions();
      initPaymentTabs();
      renderCheckoutSummary();
      window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderCheckoutSummary);
    });

    // Form Validation & Place Order
    document.getElementById('checkout-form').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      let valid = true;
      let firstInvalid = null;

      const email = document.getElementById('checkout-email');
      const phone = document.getElementById('checkout-phone');
      const firstName = document.getElementById('first-name');
      const lastName = document.getElementById('last-name');
      const address = document.getElementById('address');
      const city = document.getElementById('city');
      const state = document.getElementById('state');
      const zip = document.getElementById('zip');
      const country = document.getElementById('country');

      const emailPattern = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
      const phoneDigits = phone.value.replace(/\D/g, '');

      const checks = [
        { input: email, ok: emailPattern.test(email.value.trim()) },
        { input: phone, ok: phoneDigits.length >= 7 },
        { input: firstName, ok: firstName.value.trim().length > 0 },
        { input: lastName, ok: lastName.value.trim().length > 0 },
        { input: address, ok: address.value.trim().length > 0 },
        { input: city, ok: city.value.trim().length > 0 },
        { input: state, ok: state.value.trim().length > 0 },
        { input: zip, ok: zip.value.trim().length > 0 },
        { input: country, ok: country.value.trim().length > 0 }
      ];

      if (getSelectedPaymentMethod() === 'card') {
        const cardNum = document.getElementById('card-num');
        const cardExp = document.getElementById('card-exp');
        const cardCvc = document.getElementById('card-cvc');
        checks.push(
          { input: cardNum, ok: cardNum.value.trim().length >= 12 },
          { input: cardExp, ok: /^\d{2}\/\d{2}$/.test(cardExp.value.trim()) },
          { input: cardCvc, ok: /^\d{3,4}$/.test(cardCvc.value.trim()) }
        );
      }

      checks.forEach(({ input, ok }) => {
        if (!ok) {
          input.parentElement.classList.add('error');
          if (!firstInvalid) firstInvalid = input;
          valid = false;
        } else {
          input.parentElement.classList.remove('error');
        }
      });

      if (!valid) {
        if (firstInvalid) firstInvalid.focus();
        STRIDE_APP.showToast('Please complete all required checkout fields.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      if (valid) {
        const orderId = `STR-${Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)}`;
        const cart = STRIDE_APP.getCart();
        const subtotal = STRIDE_APP.getCartSubtotal();
        const shippingMethod = getSelectedShippingMethod();
        const shippingCost = getShippingCost(subtotal);
        const promo = STRIDE_APP.getActiveDiscount();
        const discountAmount = promo.amount || 0;
        const total = Math.max(0, subtotal + shippingCost - discountAmount);

        const newOrder = {
          orderId: orderId,
          date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }),
          status: 'Authenticated by Stride',
          total: total,
          paymentMethod: getSelectedPaymentMethod(),
          carrier: shippingMethod === 'overnight' ? 'STRIDE Armored Courier' : 'FedEx Express Priority',
          trackingNumber: `FX-${Math.floor(100000000 + Math.random() * 900000000)}-US`,
          estimatedDelivery: shippingMethod === 'overnight' ? 'Next Morning Delivery' : 'In 2 Business Days',
          currentStep: 1,
          items: cart,
          shippingAddress: {
            name: `${firstName.value} ${lastName.value}`,
            address: address.value,
            city: city.value,
            state: state.value,
            zip: zip.value,
            country: country.value.trim() || 'United States'
          }
        };

        // Save order to localStorage
        let orders = [];
        try {
          const stored = localStorage.getItem('stride_orders');
          orders = stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : [];
        } catch(e) {}
        orders.unshift(newOrder);
        localStorage.setItem('stride_orders', JSON.stringify(orders));

        // Clear cart
        localStorage.setItem('stride_cart', JSON.stringify([]));
        localStorage.removeItem('stride_promo_code');
        STRIDE_APP.updateBadges();

        // Redirect to Order Confirmation
        window.location.href = `order-confirmation.html?orderId=${orderId}`;
      }
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

1: ---
2: name: antigravity-guide
3: description: Provides a comprehensive guide, quick reference, and sitemap for Google Antigravity (AGY), including the Antigravity CLI (agy), Antigravity 2.0, Antigravity IDE, Python SDK, slash commands, keybindings, and customizations (skills, rules, MCP, sidecars). Activate this skill when the user asks questions about how to use, configure, or customize Antigravity, AGY, the agy CLI, the Antigravity IDE, or Antigravity 2.0.
4: ---
5: 
6: # Google Antigravity (AGY) Guide & Sitemap
7: 
8: Google Antigravity is an AI-first development platform. Depending on which
9: surface the user is asking about, you **MUST** read the corresponding
10: subdocumentation in the `references/` directory of this skill:
11: 
12: ## 1. Surfaces Sitemap (Offline Subdocs)
13: 
14: -   **Antigravity CLI (`agy`)**: [references/cli.md](references/cli.md)
15:     -   Pointers to the authoritative public CLI docs for slash commands,
16:         features, settings, and best practices.
17: -   **Antigravity IDE**: [references/ide.md](references/ide.md)
18:     -   Covers the standalone AI-first IDE, sidebar chat panels, and inline code
19:         lenses.
20: -   **Antigravity 2.0**: [references/app.md](references/app.md)
21:     -   Covers the parallel desktop application, left-hand sidebar, chat canvas,
22:         and the HTML Auxiliary Pane (Subagents, Background Tasks, Artifacts,
23:         Files Changed, Terminals).
24: -   **Antigravity SDK**: [references/sdk.md](references/sdk.md)
25:     -   Covers the public Python SDK
26:         (https://github.com/google-antigravity/antigravity-sdk-python) for
27:         programmatic agent leasing, orchestration APIs, and custom tool
28:         exposing.
29: 
30: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
31: 
32: ## 2. Smart Hybrid Retrieval: When to Fetch Live Docs
33: 
34: The offline subdocs provide excellent quick references. However, if the user
35: asks for the latest updates, advanced Vertex AI integrations, or complex setups
36: not covered here, you **MUST** dynamically fetch the live page from the official
37: sitemap. Every docs page has a raw Markdown mirror at the same URL with `.md`
38: appended, which is much smaller than the HTML page, so always fetch the `.md`
39: URL for docs pages:
40: 
41: <!-- LINT.IfChange(sitemap) -->
42: 
43: -   **Index of all pages**: `https://antigravity.google/llms.txt` (lists every
44:     docs page; append `.md` to a docs page URL to fetch it)
45: -   **Getting Started**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/getting-started.md`
46: -   **Skills**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/skills.md`
47: -   **Rules**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/rules.md`
48: -   **Hooks**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/hooks.md`
49: -   **Plugins**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/plugins.md`
50: -   **Sidecars**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/sidecars.md`
51: -   **Model Context Protocol (MCP)**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/mcp.md`
52: -   **Browser Automation & Testing**:
53:     `https://antigravity.google/docs/ide/browser.md`
54: -   **Agent Permissions & Security**:
55:     `https://antigravity.google/docs/permissions.md`
56: -   **Terminal Sandbox**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/sandbox.md`
57: -   **Changelog & Release Notes**:
58:     `https://antigravity.google/docs/changelog.md` (newest releases first under
59:     each product heading: `## Antigravity App`, `## Antigravity CLI`,
60:     `## IDE Extensions`, `## Antigravity SDK`, `## Antigravity IDE`). The file
61:     is large, so search the saved content for the heading of the product the
62:     user asked about and read only the first few releases under it.
63: -   **Troubleshooting & Support**: `https://antigravity.google/support` (not a
64:     docs page, so it has no `.md` mirror)
65:     <!-- LINT.ThenChange(//depot/google3/third_party/gemini_coder/agent_ui_toolkit/dev/appVariant/externalCustomLinks.ts:custom_links) -->

1: # Antigravity CLI (`agy`) Reference
2: 
3: The Antigravity CLI (`agy`) is a lightweight, terminal-based interface for fast
4: agent interaction. For full details, always consult the live public
5: documentation:
6: 
7: -   **CLI Features & Subagents**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/features.md`
8:     (see the `### Antigravity CLI` subsections)
9: -   **CLI Best Practices**:
10:     `https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/best-practices.md`
11: -   **CLI Reference**: `https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/reference.md`
12: 
13: When the user asks about CLI specifics, **fetch the relevant page above** for
14: authoritative, up-to-date information.
15: 
16: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
17: 
18: ## 1. Getting Started
19: 
20: -   **Launch**: Run `agy` to start the CLI.
21: -   **Authentication**: On first run, follow the on-screen prompts to
22:     authenticate. See `https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/reference.md` for
23:     details.
24: -   **Exit**: `Ctrl+D Ctrl+D` (or `/exit` or `/quit`).
25: 
26: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27: 
28: ## 2. CLI Slash Commands
29: 
30: -   **CLI flags & subcommands**: Run `agy --help` to see all command-line flags
31:     and subcommands.
32: -   **Slash commands** (inside the TUI): Launch `agy` and run `/help` to see all
33:     available slash commands.
34: -   **Full reference**: Fetch
35:     `https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/reference.md` for the authoritative
36:     list of all slash commands and CLI options.
37: 
38: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
39: 
40: ## 3. Configuration
41: 
42: The CLI is configured via **`~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/settings.json`**. For the
43: full list of settings keys, types, and defaults, fetch the live docs:
44: `https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/reference.md`
